Now local refinement: After SLSQP, we can do **gradient line search on exact max** or smooth. Use active-set deterministic:
Given h, C = max S. Choose active set A = indices where S >= C - tol (e.g., tol=1e-7 or 1e-6). Compute subgradient g = average of grad S_l for l in A (not softmax). Then project direction d = -g, sum zero, bounds. Use projected line search: for step t, p = project_box_sum(h + t*d, target), compute exact C, accept if decreases. This is a nonsmooth descent (subgradient) with projection. Could improve final bits. But subgradient can be noisy; use small steps and backtracking. SLSQP already does local optimization. But final pair search can squeeze. We need a fast pair search alternative.

**Exact incremental pair move**:
We can precompute S array for current h. For a candidate pair (i,j) moving δ: p_i += δ, p_j -= δ. We can compute new S quickly? Perhaps using the old S and precomputed contribution arrays.
Let e_i = unit vector at i, e_j. Change Δp = δ(e_i - e_j). New p = p + Δp.
S_l(p+Δ) = S_l(p) + lin_l(Δ) + quad_l(Δ), where lin_l = Δ dot grad S_l(p), quad = Δ_i Δ_{i+l}? More specifically only terms involving i/j. Could compute exact new S_l array in O(n) by updating contributions from i and j. But if doing many pairs, O(n) each too heavy. Could compute effect on max by evaluating only lags affected? All lags affected but change formula maybe O(1) for most? Let's derive.
S_l = ∑_{a} p_a q_{a+l}. Terms that change when p_i and p_j change:
- Terms where first index a=i: for l = b-i where b = i+l valid. Only one specific l = b-i for each b? Wait changing p_i affects S_l for **all** l where a=i and b=i+l valid: l ∈ [-i, n-1-i]. There are O(n) lags. Changing p_j affects O(n). Additionally if the two indices pair, cross terms for l=j-i and i-j.
Thus pair change updates O(n) S_l. To evaluate if max decreases, we need max over all updated S_l. New S_k for affected lags: old S_k + ΔS_k; unaffected unchanged. The max new = max(current max among unaffected, max affected new). We can precompute global max and count of max maybe. Delta arrays length O(n); to compute max affected efficiently O(n). So pair candidate O(n). For all pairs O(n^3).
Can we derive ΔS_k in closed form O(1) per k? For a single pair, yes but still O(n) to compute max over k unless we use precomputed structure? Maybe not.

But we can use gradient line search to pick directions instead of all pairs.

Pipeline idea:
1. Setup t0, deadline. Load parent h_init. Project to feasible. Evaluate c.
2. Create initial candidate list:
   - parent unsym at n_parent.
   - parent sym at n_parent (h_sym=(p+rev)/2, project; extract half).
   - For each n in [200? maybe parent, 300, 400, 500?], interpolate parent to n, project. For sym if n even.
   - Maybe a constructed low-dimensional pattern? Since parent good, start from it.
3. For each candidate, run `optimize_candidate(h, n, symmetric, time_alloc)`:
   - h initial full or x half.
   - Phase1 SLSQP with alpha [50, 200, 1000, 5000] maybe maxiter proportional to dimension/time. For each alpha, run SLSQP from previous best; if exact C improves, update. Use options maxiter maybe 300-800, ftol 1e-12. Need avoid too long. Could pass `maxiter=500` for small dims, `ftol=1e-12`, `disp=False`.
   - After SLSQP, do a **projected smooth gradient descent polish**: Use high alpha gradient and backtracking line search for exact C, maybe 100 steps.
4. Keep global best (h_full,c,n).

Function:
```
def gradient_weights_full(h, n, weights):
    m=2*n-1
    # A_total
    starts = n-1 - np.arange(n)
    ends = 2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
    ps = np.empty(m+1); ps[0]=0; np.cumsum(weights, out=ps[1:])
    A_total = ps[ends+1] - ps[starts]
    conv_w = np.convolve(h, weights, mode='full')
    conv_wr = np.convolve(h, weights[::-1], mode='full')
    idx = n-1 + np.arange(n)
    A_p = conv_wr[idx]
    B_p = conv_w[idx]
    return A_total - A_p - B_p
```

```
def resample_to_n(h, n):
    n_old=len(h)
```

_Log Smooth value:
```
def _smooth_value_full(h,n,alpha):
    h = np.asarray(h,float)
    # if tiny out clip
    if h.min() < 0 or h.max()>1: h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    dx=2.0/n
    S=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')*dx
    sh=alpha*(S-S.max())
    # prevent overflow (sh<=0)
    return float(S.max() + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh)))/alpha)
```
If alpha=0? no.

```
def _smooth_grad_full(h,n,alpha):

    if s<=0 or not finite: w=np.zeros; w[argmax(S)]=1
    else: w/=s
    # maybe zero tiny
    w[w < 1e-15]=0.0
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws >0: w/=ws  # ensure sum 1 for gradient? This changes exact gradient but fine.
    else: w[:-?]
    return gradient_weights_full(h,n,w)
```

```
def gradient_weights_full(h,n,weights):
    L=2*n-1
    weights=np.asarray(weights,float).ravel()
    if weights.size != L: ...
    # cumulative
    ps=np.empty(L+1); ps[0]=0; np.cumsum(weights,out=ps[1:])
    idx=np.arange(n)
    starts=n-1-idx; ends=idx? ends = 2*n-2 - idx
    At=ps[ends+1]-ps[starts]
    conv=np.convolve(h, weights, mode='full')
    convr=np.convolve(h, weights[::-1], mode='full')
    off=np.arange(n-1, n-1+n)
    return At - convr[off] - conv[off]
```

```
def evaluate_erdos_full(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return float('inf')
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)
```

`project_box_sum(z,target)`:
Implement robust. Let's code:
```
def project_box_sum(z, target):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float).copy()
    n=z.size
    if n==0: return z
    target=float(target)
    if target <= 0.0: return np.zeros(n)
    if target >= n: return np.ones(n)
    # first clip? Bisection can handle infinities? replace nan
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    lo = float(np.min(z)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(z))
    # We want sum(clip(z-lambda)) decreasing with lambda. At hi maybe sum=count of max? Actually if lambda=hi, values >=hi? z-hi <=0, clip0 =>0. If multiple max, clip of 0? 0. Good sum 0. At lo=min-1, z-lo >=0; min value ->1, others >1->1, sum n. Good.
    # ensure target between 0,n. Bisection
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.clip(z-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s > target:
           lo=mid
       else:
           hi=mid
    p=np.clip(z-hi,0.0,1.0)
    # correction
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-p.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-p
          tot=room.sum()
          if tot < 1e-15: break
          p += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          base=p
          tot=base.sum()
          if tot < 1e-15: break
          p += (diff/tot)*base
       p=np.clip(p,0.0,1.0)
    return p
```
The correction with proportional can still leave residual if repeated; 100 enough. For sym target m/2 maybe target not integer; okay.

```
def resample_full(h,n_new):
    n_old=len(h)
    if n_old==n_new: return h.copy()
    if n_new<2: n_new=2
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
```

```
def _smooth_value_counts(h,n,alpha):
   S=_S_counts(h,n)
   M=float(S.max())

   # For extremely large alpha, sh may be -inf due underflow? exp(-inf)=0.
```

```
def optimize_sym(h0,n,t_start,window,alphas,maxiter):
    m=n//2
    h=(h0+h0[::-1])*0.5
    h=project_box_sum(h, n/2.0)
    x=h[:m].copy()
    x=project_box_sum(x, n/4.0)
    full=full_from_sym(x,n); c=evaluate(full)
    best_x=x.copy(); best_c=c
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':constraint_sum,'args':(n/4.0,),'jac':constraint_sum_jac}]
    prev_fun=None
    for alpha in alphas:
       rem = t_start+window - time.time()
       if rem < 3: break
       mx = max(50, min(maxiter, int(rem/0.05))) # assume 0.05/iter? maybe too many. Use min(maxiter, 800 if m<=100 else 400)
       try:
           res=minimize(obj_smooth_sym,x,args=(n,alpha),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,
                        jac=jac_smooth_sym,options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':1e-12})
           x_new=res.x if res.x is not None else x
       except: x_new=x
       x_new=project_box_sum(x_new, n/4.0)
       full_new=full_from_sym(x_new,n); c_new=evaluate(full_new)
       if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
           best_c=c_new; best_x=x_new.copy()
       # choose next x: if c_new <= best_c + 1e-6 or res.fun < prev_fun? use x_new
       if c_new <= best_c + 1e-5:
           x=x_new
       else:
           x=best_x.copy()
    # final high alpha maybe already in list
    full=full_from_sym(best_x,n); return full, evaluate(full)
```

```
    elif n_vars <= 150: cap=600

    else: cap=200
```

Now, final polish function. Since we use counts, gradient. Implement:
```
def polish_projected(h,n,deadline,alpha=800,maxsteps=200):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n/2)
    best_c=evaluate(h)
    target=n/2
    no_improve=0
    for _ in range(maxsteps):
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem<2: break
       g=_smooth_grad_counts(h,n,alpha)
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if not finite or gmax<1e-12: break
       d=-g/gmax
       improved=False
       steps=[0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005]
       for s in steps:
           xp=project_box_sum(h+s*d, target)
           c=evaluate(xp)
           if c < best_c - 1e-14:
               h=xp; best_c=c; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           # try smaller step in random? break after 3
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve>=5: break
       else: no_improve=0
       # occasionally lower step? Not necessary
    return h,best_c
```
This may be slow evaluating each candidate exactly O(n^2) in C. 200 steps *7 evals *n=200 (80k) ~112M C, maybe 1s. OK. But if no improvement quickly break. It may get stuck due projection sum; maybe okay.

`pair_delta_max_only(h,n,i,j,delta,S)`:
If bounds fail return inf. Loop lags and compute max, no delta array. But if want update, need delta array. We'll write a function `pair_move_counts(h,n,i,j,delta,S)` returns `(new_max, delta_arr)` maybe. For candidate best delta, we can recompute. To avoid twice per delta, we can compute delta_arr and new_max together:
```
def pair_move_counts(h,n,i,j,delta,S):
    pi=h[i]; pj=h[j]; pin=pi+delta; pjn=pj-delta
    if pin<0 or pin>1 or pjn<0 or pjn>1: return None
    d=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    L=2*n-1
    # loop idx 0..L-1
    for idx in range(L):
       lag=idx-(n-1)
       # candidate a list unique
       a=-10**9? We'll handle terms:
       total=0.0
       # term a=i if i+lag valid
       if 0 <= i+lag:
           # automatically i+lag < n?
           if i+lag < n:
               a=i; b=i+lag
               va=h[a]; vb=h[b]
               if a==i: van=pin
               elif a==j: van=pjn
               else: van=va
               if b==i: vbn=pin
               elif b==j: vbn=pjn
               else: vbn=vb
               total += van*(1.0-vbn) - va*(1.0-vb)
       # term a=j
       if j!=i and 0 <= j+lag < n:
           a=j; b=j+lag
           ... total += ...
       # term with i as second: a=i-lag, b=i
       a2=i-lag
       if 0 <= a2 < n:
           # b=i valid
           if a2 != i and a2 != j: # if a2 same as i? then lag=0, term a=i already handled. If a2=j? then term first j? maybe handled if j+lag=i? That's cross lag i-j; handled by term a=j if valid. skip.
               a=a2; b=i
               ...
       a3=j-lag
       if 0 <= a3 < n:
           if a3 != i and a3 != j:
               a=a3; b=j
               ...
       d[idx]=total
    new_max = float(np.max(S+d))
    return new_max,d
```
Need avoid double counting lag=0 term for i as second a=i (lag0) already term a=i; skip if a2==i. Similarly if a2==j handles cross where first j second i, term a=j first if j+lag=i valid (lag=i-j); yes skip if a2==j to avoid duplicate. What if a2 equals i or j but term a=i/j not valid? If a2=i implies lag=0, term a=i valid (i+0 valid) yes. If a2=j means first j second i, term a=j valid if j+lag=n? lag=i-j, j+lag=i valid yes. So skip.
What if candidate a from i-as-second equals candidate a from j-as-second? Both b different i/j? Could a2=a3 => i-lag=j-lag => i=j, no. Could a2 equals j-as-first? handled.
What if term a=i and term a=j same? i=j no.
Thus unique.

But we also need terms where coordinate i is second and **also** first for the same lag? lag=0, handled by a=i; skip second.
For cross lag l=i-j, term a=j first and i second (a2=j) duplicate skip. For lag l=j-i, term a=i first and j second (a3=i) skip if a3==i.
Good.

For candidate generation, instead of evaluating all deltas by computing full delta array for each, we can compute once per pair/delta. Fine.

Potential bug: old contribution `va*(1-vb)` for term where a or b is the other coordinate uses old values. Good.
New values using pin/pjn if a/b corresponds.
If lag such that b out of range, term invalid. We check for a=i first: 0<=i+lag<n. For a2=i-lag, b=i valid if 0<=a2<n; also need a2+lag=i within (yes) and a2 first valid? Yes if a2 valid. For lag negative, a2 maybe >? fine.
For a=j-lag similar.

`pair_descent(h,n,deadline,rng,max_rounds=12,K=12,deltas=...,alpha=500)`:
```
def pair_descent(h,n,deadline,rng,max_rounds=12,K=12,alpha=500):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n/2)
    S=_S_counts(h,n)
    best_count=float(S.max())
    best_c=best_count*2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy()
    deltas=[0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002]
    stale=0
    for rnd in range(max_rounds):
        if time.time()>deadline-2: break
        w=_softmax_weights_counts(S,alpha)
        g=_gradient_weights(h,n,w)
        # candidate pairs
        if K >= n: i_list=np.arange(n); j_list=np.arange(n)
        else:
            i_idx=np.argsort(g)[:K]
            j_idx=np.argsort(g)[-K:][::-1]
        cand=[]
        for i in i_list:
           for j in j_list:
              if i!=j: cand.append((int(i),int(j),1)) # increase i
              # maybe also add opposite if g signs not strict?
        # limit pairs: if len > 120 random sample
        max_pairs=200
        if len(cand)>max_pairs:
           cand=[cand[i] for i in rng.choice(len(cand), max_pairs, replace=False)]
        improved_any=False
        for (i,j,sign) in cand:
           if time.time()>deadline-2: break
           # sign=+1 means delta positive for i; if we want opposite, could evaluate -1 if sign? We only choose i low g, j high, so sign=1.
           # Try deltas; for speed first delta 0.005; if no improve try smaller.
           # Actually to find best, evaluate all deltas and pick.
           best_delta=None; best_new_inf=best_count
           for d in deltas:
              move=pair_move_counts(h,n,i,j,d,S)
              if move is None: continue
              new_max, delta_arr = move
              if new_max < best_new_inf:
                  best_new_inf=new_max; best_delta=(d,delta_arr)
           if best_delta is not None and best_delta[0]? and best_new_inf < best_count - 1e-12:
              d=best_delta[0]; delta_arr=best_delta[1]
              h[i]+=d; h[j]-=d
              h=project_box_sum(h,n/2) # to correct rounding
              S=_S_counts(h,n) # recompute exact
              best_count=float(S.max())
              best_c=best_count*2.0/n
              best_h=h.copy()
              improved_any=True
              # If time, continue with same S/g? g stale; maybe break round and recompute? For simplicity break to recompute gradient after some improves? We can continue but gradient stale. We can limit number accepts per round e.g. 10.
              if ...
        if not improved_any:
           stale+=1
           if stale>=4: break
        else: stale=0
```

```
def _slsqp_maxiter(nvars, rem, cap):
    if nvars <= 80: hard=1200

    estimate=int(rem/0.05)
    return int(max(50, min(hard, cap, estimate)))
```

Let's write `_choose_pair_candidates(g,n,rng,K,max_pairs)`:
```
def _choose_pair_candidates(g,n,rng,K,max_pairs):
    L=[]
    order=np.argsort(g)
    low=order[:K]
    high=order[-K:][::-1]
    for i in low:
       for j in high:
          if i!=j: L.append((int(i),int(j)))
    # if len < max_pairs, add random
    while len(L) < max_pairs:
       i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
       if i!=j:
          d = 1 if g[i] <= g[j] else -1? We need orientation. We can store tuple i_get (increase) and j_give.
          if g[i] <= g[j]: L.append((i,j)) else L.append((j,i))
    # add nearby gradient-ordered pairs
    for r in [1,2,3,5,10]:
       if r<n:
          for i in range(0,n-r, max(1,n//50)?):
             a=i; b=i+r
             if g[a] <= g[b]: L.append((a,b)) else L.append((b,a))
             if len(L) > max_pairs*2: break
          if len(L)>max_pairs*2: break
    if len(L)>max_pairs:
       idxs=rng.choice(len(L), max_pairs, replace=False)
       L=[L[int(k)] for k in idxs]
    return L
```

Now, pair descent candidate generation and move. Let's code carefully.

`_pair_candidates(g,n,rng,K,max_pairs)`:
```
def _pair_candidates(g,n,rng,K,max_pairs):
    cand=[]
    if n < 2: return cand
    order=np.argsort(g)
    K=min(K,n//2)
    low=order[:K]
    high=order[-K:][::-1]
    for ii in low:
       for jj in high:
          if ii != jj:
             if g[ii] <= g[jj]: cand.append((int(ii),int(jj)))
             else: cand.append((int(jj),int(ii)))
    # nearby
    rlist=[1,2,3,5,10,20]
    for r in rlist:
       if r >= n: continue
       step=max(1,n//80)
       for i in range(0,n-r,step):
          a=i; b=i+r
          if g[a] <= g[b]: cand.append((a,b))
          else: cand.append((b,a))
          if len(cand) > max_pairs: break
       if len(cand) > max_pairs: break
    # random if not enough or to fill variety
    while len(cand) < max_pairs:
       a=int(rng.randint(n)); b=int(rng.randint(n))
       if a!=b:
          if g[a] <= g[b]: cand.append((a,b))
          else: cand.append((b,a))
    if len(cand) > max_pairs:
       idxs=np.random.choice? use rng.choice
       idxs=rng.choice(len(cand), max_pairs, replace=False)
       cand=[cand[int(k)] for k in idxs]
    return cand
```
Note if low and high same due small K, orientation may create pairs with g not lower? We orient by g. Good.

`pair_move_counts` with delta positive. Let's code with local variables for speed. Use `range_L = range(2*n-1)`? In loop, computing lag = idx - (n-1). Could precompute `lags = np.arange(-(n-1), n)`; but in pair_move called repeatedly; we can pass lags? To avoid allocate, generate in run and pass? Top-level? We can compute inside: `lags = np.arange(-(n-1), n, dtype=int)` each call (length 400) okay but to reduce, we can define `n1=n-1` and use lag variable. Python for idx in range(L): lag=idx-n1.
Inside per candidate fill `d[idx]=total`. Use if statements. Need h length n. Use `h_i=h[i]`, `h_j=h[j]`, `pi_new`, `pj_new`.

Pseudo:
```
def pair_move_counts(h,n,i,j,delta,S):
    pi=h[i]; pj=h[j]
    pin=pi+delta; pjn=pj-delta
    if pin < 0.0 or pin > 1.0 or pjn < 0.0 or pjn > 1.0:
        return None
    L=2*n-1
    d=np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    n1=n-1
    for idx in range(L):
       lag=idx-n1
       total=0.0
       # first i
       b0=i+lag
       if 0 <= b0 < n:
           a=i; b=b0
           va=h[a]; vb=h[b]
           if a == i: van=pin
           elif a == j: van=pjn
           else: van=va
           if b == i: vbn=pin
           elif b == j: vbn=pjn
           else: vbn=vb
           total += van*(1.0-vbn) - va*(1.0-vb)
       # first j
       if j != i:
          b1=j+lag
          if 0 <= b1 < n:
             a=j; b=b1
             ... total +=
       # second i
       a2=i-lag
       if 0 <= a2 < n:
          if a2 != i and a2 != j:
             b=i
             va=h[a2]; vb=h[b]
             van=va (a2 not i/j)
             if b == i: vbn=pin
             elif b == j: vbn=pjn
             else: vbn=vb
             total += van*(1-vbn) - va*(1-vb)
       # second j
       a3=j-lag
       if 0 <= a3 < n:
          if a3 != i and a3 != j:
             b=j
             va=h[a3]; vb=h[b]
             van=va
             if b == j: vbn=pjn
             elif b == i: vbn=pin
             else: vbn=vb
             total += van*(1-vbn) - va*(1-vb)
       d[idx]=total
    new_max = float(np.max(S + d))
    return new_max, d
```
For second i, b=i; vbn should be pin, no j. For second j, b=j, vbn=pjn. Good.

`pair_descent`:
```
def pair_descent(h,n,deadline,rng,alpha=300.0,max_rounds=15,K=12,max_pairs=120,deltas=None):
    if deltas is None: deltas=[0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002]
    h=project_box_sum(h,n/2)
    S=_S_counts(h,n)
    best_count=float(S.max())
    best_c=best_count*(2.0/n)
    best_h=h.copy()
    stale=0
    for rnd in range(max_rounds):
       if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
       w=_smooth_weights_counts(S,alpha)
       g=_gradient_weights(h,n,w)
       cands=_pair_candidates(g,n,rng,K,max_pairs)
       improved_any=False
       accepts=0
       for (i,j) in cands:
          if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
          if accepts >= 5: break
          for d in deltas:
             move=pair_move_counts(h,n,i,j,d,S)
             if move is None: continue
             new_max, delt = move
             if new_max < best_count - 1e-12:
                # apply
                h[i]+=d; h[j]-=d
                h=project_box_sum(h,n/2)
                S=_S_counts(h,n)
                new_max=float(S.max())
                best_count=new_max
                best_c=best_count*(2.0/n)
                best_h=h.copy()
                accepts +=1
                improved_any=True
                break
          # after update, previous cands gradient stale; but still okay until accepts limit
       if not improved_any:
          stale +=1
          if stale >= 4: break
       else: stale=0
    return best_h,best_c
```

Potential: `pair_move_counts` for d=0.02 might improve, but a larger d maybe better; we don't explore after first improvement. Could miss more. But subsequent rounds will try again. Fine.

Could if first feasible d not improve but smaller improve; our loop tries descending. Good.

```
def _slsqp_maxiter(nvars, remaining, cap):
    if nvars <= 80: hard=1200; tp=0.02
    elif nvars <= 150: hard=700; tp=0.03
    elif nvars <= 250: hard=400; tp=0.05
    else: hard=250; tp=0.08
    estimate=int(remaining/tp)
    val=min(hard, int(cap), estimate)
    if val < 30: val=30 if remaining>2 else 10
    return int(val)
```

```
def resample_full(h,n_new):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n_old=h.size
    if n_old == n_new: return h.copy()
    if n_old == 1: return np.full(n_new,h[0])
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
    return y
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

np.seterr(over="ignore", under="ignore", divide="ignore", invalid="ignore")


def evaluate_erdos_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def _S_counts(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def project_box_sum(z, target):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).copy()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return z
    target = float(target)
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)

    lo = float(np.min(z)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(z))
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(z - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    p = np.clip(z - hi, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(120):
        diff = target - float(p.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - p
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            p += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            base = p
            tot = float(base.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            p += (diff / tot) * base
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0)
    return p


def full_from_sym(x, n):
    m = n // 2
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    full = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    full[:m] = x
    full[m:] = x[::-1]
    return full


def resample_full(h, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n_old = h.size
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return h
    if n_new < 2:
        n_new = 2
    if n_old == 1:
        return np.full(n_new, float(h[0]), dtype=float)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)


def constraint_sum(x, target):
    return float(np.sum(x) - float(target))


def constraint_sum_jac(x, target):
    return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)


def _smooth_value_counts(h, n, alpha):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        h = h[:n]
    if h.size < n:
        h = np.pad(h, (0, n - h.size), mode="edge")
    if (h < 0.0).any() or (h > 1.0).any():
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    S = _S_counts(h, n)
    M = float(S.max())
    sh = alpha * (S - M)
    sh = np.maximum(sh, -745.0)
    s = float(np.exp(sh).sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return M
    return M + float(np.log(s)) / alpha


def _smooth_weights_counts(S, alpha):
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=float)
    M = float(S.max())
    sh = alpha * (S - M)
    sh = np.maximum(sh, -745.0)
    w = np.exp(sh)
    w[sh < -30.0] = 0.0
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.zeros_like(S)
        w[int(np.argmax(S))] = 1.0
        return w
    w /= ws
    return w


def _gradient_weights(h, n, weights):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if weights.size != L:
        weights = np.pad(weights, (0, max(0, L - weights.size)), mode="constant")[:L]
    nz = np.flatnonzero(weights > 0.0)
    if nz.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    if nz.size < 80:
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        n1 = n - 1
        for idx in nz:
            wr = float(weights[idx])
            if wr == 0.0:
                continue
            lag = int(idx) - n1
            if lag >= 0:
                m = n - lag
                if m > 0:
                    g[:m] += wr * (1.0 - h[lag:lag + m])
                    g[lag:] -= wr * h[:m]
            else:
                d = -lag
                m = n - d
                if m > 0:
                    g[d:] += wr * (1.0 - h[:m])
                    g[:m] -= wr * h[:m]
        return g

    ps = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=float)
    ps[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(weights, out=ps[1:])

    idx = np.arange(n)
    starts = n - 1 - idx
    ends = 2 * n - 2 - idx
    A_total = ps[ends + 1] - ps[starts]

    conv_w = np.convolve(h, weights, mode="full")
    conv_wr = np.convolve(h, weights[::-1], mode="full")
    off = n - 1 + idx
    return A_total - conv_wr[off] - conv_w[off]


def _smooth_grad_counts(h, n, alpha):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        h = h[:n]
    if h.size < n:
        h = np.pad(h, (0, n - h.size), mode="edge")
    if (h < 0.0).any() or (h > 1.0).any():
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    S = _S_counts(h, n)
    w = _smooth_weights_counts(S, alpha)
    return _gradient_weights(h, n, w)


def obj_smooth_unsym(x, n, alpha):
    return _smooth_value_counts(x, int(n), float(alpha))


def jac_smooth_unsym(x, n, alpha):
    return _smooth_grad_counts(x, int(n), float(alpha))


def obj_smooth_sym(x, n, alpha):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    m = n // 2
    x = x[:m]
    if (x < 0.0).any() or (x > 1.0).any():
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    full = full_from_sym(x, n)
    return _smooth_value_counts(full, n, float(alpha))


def jac_smooth_sym(x, n, alpha):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    m = n // 2
    x = x[:m]
    if (x < 0.0).any() or (x > 1.0).any():
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    full = full_from_sym(x, n)
    gfull = _smooth_grad_counts(full, n, float(alpha))
    return gfull[:m] + gfull[n - 1 - np.arange(m)]


def _slsqp_maxiter(nvars, remaining, cap):
    if nvars <= 80:
        hard = 1200
        tp = 0.02
    elif nvars <= 150:
        hard = 700
        tp = 0.03
    elif nvars <= 250:
        hard = 400
        tp = 0.05
    else:
        hard = 250
        tp = 0.08
    estimate = int(max(0.0, remaining) / max(1e-6, tp))
    val = min(int(hard), int(cap), estimate)
    if val < 25:
        val = 25 if remaining > 1.0 else 10
    return int(val)


def optimize_unsym(h0, n, deadline, alphas, maxiter):
    n = int(n)
    h = resample_full(h0, n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, n / 2.0)
    x = h.copy()
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = evaluate_erdos_full(x)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    target = n / 2.0
    cons = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": constraint_sum,
            "args": (target,),
            "jac": constraint_sum_jac,
        }
    ]

    for alpha in alphas:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 4.0:
            break
        mx = _slsqp_maxiter(n, rem, maxiter)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_smooth_unsym,
                x,
                args=(n, float(alpha)),
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                jac=jac_smooth_unsym,
                options={"maxiter": mx, "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": False},
            )
            x_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if x_new.size != n:
                x_new = x
            x_new = project_box_sum(x_new, target)
            c_new = evaluate_erdos_full(x_new)
            if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c_new
                best_x = x_new.copy()
            if c_new <= best_c + 1e-6:
                x = x_new.copy()
            else:
                x = best_x.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_x = project_box_sum(best_x, target)
    return best_x, evaluate_erdos_full(best_x)


def optimize_sym(h0, n, deadline, alphas, maxiter):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0 or n < 4:
        return optimize_unsym(h0, n, deadline, alphas, maxiter)

    m = n // 2
    h = resample_full(h0, n)
    hs = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    hs = project_box_sum(hs, n / 2.0)

    x = hs[:m].copy()
    target = n / 4.0
    x = project_box_sum(x, target)

    full0 = full_from_sym(x, n)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = evaluate_erdos_full(full0)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
    cons = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": constraint_sum,
            "args": (target,),
            "jac": constraint_sum_jac,
        }
    ]

    for alpha in alphas:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 4.0:
            break
        mx = _slsqp_maxiter(m, rem, maxiter)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_smooth_sym,
                x,
                args=(n, float(alpha)),
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                jac=jac_smooth_sym,
                options={"maxiter": mx, "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": False},
            )
            x_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if x_new.size != m:
                x_new = x
            x_new = project_box_sum(x_new, target)
            full_new = full_from_sym(x_new, n)
            c_new = evaluate_erdos_full(full_new)
            if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c_new
                best_x = x_new.copy()
            if c_new <= best_c + 1e-6:
                x = x_new.copy()
            else:
                x = best_x.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_x = project_box_sum(best_x, target)
    full_best = full_from_sym(best_x, n)
    return full_best, evaluate_erdos_full(full_best)


def polish_projected(h, n, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, n / 2.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    best_c = evaluate_erdos_full(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    steps = [0.03, 0.012, 0.0045, 0.0015, 0.0005]
    alphas = [250.0, 900.0]
    total_steps = 0

    for alpha in alphas:
        no_improve = 0
        while total_steps < 60 and time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
            g = _smooth_grad_counts(h, n, alpha)
            gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
            if gmax <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(gmax):
                break
            d = -g / gmax
            improved = False
            for s in steps:
                xp = project_box_sum(h + s * d, target)
                c = evaluate_erdos_full(xp)
                if c < best_c - 1e-14:
                    h = xp
                    best_h = xp.copy()
                    best_c = c
                    total_steps += 1
                    improved = True
                    break
            if not improved:
                xp = project_box_sum(h + 1.5e-4 * d, target)
                c = evaluate_erdos_full(xp)
                if c < best_c - 1e-14:
                    h = xp
                    best_h = xp.copy()
                    best_c = c
                    total_steps += 1
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                    if no_improve >= 4:
                        break
            if total_steps >= 60:
                break
    return best_h, best_c


def pair_move_counts(h, n, i, j, delta, S):
    pi = float(h[i])
    pj = float(h[j])
    pin = pi + delta
    pjn = pj - delta
    if pin < 0.0 or pin > 1.0 or pjn < 0.0 or pjn > 1.0:
        return None

    L = 2 * n - 1
    d = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    n1 = n - 1

    for idx in range(L):
        lag = idx - n1
        total = 0.0

        b0 = i + lag
        if 0 <= b0 < n:
            a = i
            b = b0
            va = h[a]
            vb = h[b]
            van = pin if a == i else (pjn if a == j else va)
            vbn = pin if b == i else (pjn if b == j else vb)
            total += van * (1.0 - vbn) - va * (1.0 - vb)

        if j != i:
            b1 = j + lag
            if 0 <= j + lag < n:
                a = j
                b = b1
                va = h[a]
                vb = h[b]
                van = pin if a == i else (pjn if a == j else va)
                vbn = pin if b == i else (pjn if b == j else vb)
                total += van * (1.0 - vbn) - va * (1.0 - vb)

        a2 = i - lag
        if 0 <= a2 < n and a2 != i and a2 != j:
            a = a2
            b = i
            va = h[a]
            vb = h[b]
            van = va
            vbn = pin if b == i else (pjn if b == j else vb)
            total += van * (1.0 - vbn) - va * (1.0 - vb)

        a3 = j - lag
        if 0 <= a3 < n and a3 != i and a3 != j:
            a = a3
            b = j
            va = h[a]
            vb = h[b]
            van = va
            vbn = pin if b == i else (pjn if b == j else vb)
            total += van * (1.0 - vbn) - va * (1.0 - vb)

        d[idx] = total

    new_max = float(np.max(S + d))
    return new_max, d


def _pair_candidates(g, n, rng, K, max_pairs):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return []
    cand = []
    K = int(max(4, min(K, n // 2)))
    order = np.argsort(g)
    low = order[:K]
    high = order[-K:][::-1]

    for ii in low:
        ii = int(ii)
        for jj in high:
            jj = int(jj)
            if ii == jj:
                continue
            if g[ii] <= g[jj]:
                cand.append((ii, jj))
            else:
                cand.append((jj, ii))

    for r in [1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 20]:
        if r >= n:
            continue
        step = max(1, n // 80)
        for i in range(0, n - r, step):
            a = int(i)
            b = int(i + r)
            if g[a] <= g[b]:
                cand.append((a, b))
            else:
                cand.append((b, a))
            if len(cand) > max_pairs:
                break
        if len(cand) > max_pairs:
            break

    while len(cand) < max_pairs:
        a = int(rng.randint(n))
        b = int(rng.randint(n))
        if a == b:
            continue
        if g[a] <= g[b]:
            cand.append((a, b))
        else:
            cand.append((b, a))

    if len(cand) > max_pairs:
        idxs = rng.choice(len(cand), max_pairs, replace=False)
        cand = [cand[int(k)] for k in idxs]
    return cand


def pair_descent(h, n, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, n / 2.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    S = _S_counts(h, n)
    best_count = float(S.max())
    best_c = best_count * (2.0 / n)
    best_h = h.copy()

    deltas = [0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002]
    stale = 0
    max_rounds = 18

    for _ in range(max_rounds):
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        w = _smooth_weights_counts(S, 350.0)
        g = _gradient_weights(h, n, w)
        cands = _pair_candidates(g, n, rng, K=14, max_pairs=140)
        improved_any = False
        accepts = 0

        for i, j in cands:
            if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            if accepts >= 6:
                break
            for dval in deltas:
                mv = pair_move_counts(h, n, i, j, dval, S)
                if mv is None:
                    continue
                new_max, _ = mv
                if new_max < best_count - 1e-12:
                    h[i] += dval
                    h[j] -= dval
                    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
                    S = _S_counts(h, n)
                    best_count = float(S.max())
                    best_c = best_count * (2.0 / n)
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    accepts += 1
                    improved_any = True
                    break

        if not improved_any:
            stale += 1
            if stale >= 4:
                break
        else:
            stale = 0

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(budget_s, 30.0) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    try:
        p = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        p = None

    if p is None or p.size < 2:
        p = np.full(200, 0.5, dtype=float)

    n_parent = int(p.size)
    if n_parent % 2 == 1:
        n_parent += 1
    if n_parent < 100:
        n_parent = 200
    if n_parent > 500:
        n_parent = 400

    if n_parent != len(p):
        p = np.interp(
            np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_parent),
            np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(p)),
            p,
        )
    h_base = project_box_sum(p, n_parent / 2.0)

    best_h = h_base.copy()
    best_i = int(len(best_h))
    best_c = evaluate_erdos_full(best_h)

    def update_if_better(h_new, c_new):
        nonlocal_best = {}
        return h_new, c_new

    # Base resolution, symmetric and unsymmetric.
    if time.time() < deadline - 140.0:
        win = min(150.0, max(25.0, deadline - time.time() - 120.0))
        dd = min(deadline, time.time() + win)
        try:
            h_new, c_new = optimize_sym(
                h_base, n_parent, dd, [2.0, 8.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 900.0], 900
            )
            if c_new < best_c:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = h_new.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 140.0:
        win = min(140.0, max(25.0, deadline - time.time() - 120.0))
        dd = min(deadline, time.time() + win)
        try:
            h_new, c_new = optimize_unsym(
                h_base, n_parent, dd, [2.0, 8.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 900.0], 700
            )
            if c_new < best_c:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = h_new.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    h_work = best_h.copy()

    extra_ns = []
    if n_parent <= 300 and 300 != n_parent:
        extra_ns.append(300)
    if n_parent <= 400 and 400 != n_parent:
        extra_ns.append(400)

    for nt in extra_ns:
        if time.time() > deadline - 180.0:
            break
        h0 = resample_full(h_work, nt)
        h0 = project_box_sum(h0, nt / 2.0)
        win = min(120.0, max(20.0, deadline - time.time() - 140.0))
        dd = min(deadline, time.time() + win)
        try:
            h_new, c_new = optimize_sym(
                h0, nt, dd, [5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 900.0], 650
            )
            if c_new < best_c:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = h_new.copy()
                h_work = h_new.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

        if nt <= 300 and time.time() < deadline - 150.0:
            win = min(50.0, max(15.0, deadline - time.time() - 120.0))
            dd = min(deadline, time.time() + win)
            try:
                h_new, c_new = optimize_unsym(
                    h0, nt, dd, [20.0, 80.0, 300.0], 350
                )
                if c_new < best_c:
                    best_c = c_new
                    best_h = h_new.copy()
                    h_work = h_new.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    # A few modest restarts from the current best.
    if time.time() < deadline - 150.0:
        n_best = int(best_h.size)
        for scale in [0.03, 0.08, 0.15]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 90.0:
                break
            hp = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n_best)
            hp = project_box_sum(hp, n_best / 2.0)
            win = min(28.0, max(12.0, deadline - time.time() - 80.0))
            dd = min(deadline, time.time() + win)
            try:
                if n_best % 2 == 0:
                    h_new, c_new = optimize_sym(hp, n_best, dd, [15.0, 60.0, 250.0], 320)
                    if c_new < best_c:
                        best_c = c_new
                        best_h = h_new.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass
            if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
                try:
                    h_new, c_new = optimize_unsym(hp, n_best, dd, [15.0, 60.0, 250.0], 260)
                    if c_new < best_c:
                        best_c = c_new
                        best_h = h_new.copy()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Final smooth polish.
    if time.time() < deadline - 45.0:
        try:
            dd_pol = min(deadline, time.time() + 50.0)
            best_h, best_c = polish_projected(best_h, int(best_h.size), dd_pol)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Exact pair-move refinement.
    if time.time() < deadline - 10.0 and best_h.size <= 500:
        try:
            best_h, best_c = pair_descent(best_h, int(best_h.size), deadline, rng)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # One more very short SLSQP polish if time permits.
    if time.time() < deadline - 20.0 and best_h.size <= 400:
        try:
            win = min(25.0, max(8.0, deadline - time.time() - 8.0))
            dd = min(deadline, time.time() + win)
            if best_h.size % 2 == 0:
                h_new, c_new = optimize_sym(best_h, int(best_h.size), dd, [60.0, 250.0, 800.0], 260)
            else:
                h_new, c_new = optimize_unsym(best_h, int(best_h.size), dd, [60.0, 250.0, 800.0], 260)
            if c_new < best_c:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = h_new.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, int(best_h.size) / 2.0)
    best_c = evaluate_erdos_full(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_h.size)
```
